Another idea: **Convex Maximization subproblem with target M** can be solved exactly by **water filling / multiplicative weights** and used to get an exact update.   T p = cross_corr(w,p). Set p_i ← normalize( sqrt(p_i * T_i?) )? For mirror descent, if desired condition T_i = c * p_i? Actually gradient w.r.t p: 2 T_i. Natural gradient p*(2T_i - 2c). The natural gradient direction is p_i(g_i - dot(p,g_i)) = p_i(T_i - avg_weighted). This is equivalent to multiplicative update p_i ← p_i * T_i?   w = softmax(β q(p))
  K_w p? Then p_new = normalize(K_w p) (power) instead of exponential? Because K_w p is gradient/2. At optimum K_w p constant -> unchanged. Thus algorithm:
- Given p, q, w active.
- T = cross_corr(w,p)
- avg = dot(p,T) (or mean over support)
- ratio = avg/T (maybe clipped)
- θ update = -lr * log(ratio) = lr * (log T - log avg?) Actually log-space step for p_i * = normalize(p_i * ratio_i^α). θ_new = θ + α log(ratio_i). Since T high ratio low negative. This is equivalent to multiplicative gradient with g=T. We can simply use natural gradient as previous. We can choose adaptive α.

Let's design final program with careful structure:

Top-level functions:
- `fft_conv_score_grad(p, Nq, Nw, beta)` computes q and gradient naturally.
- `softmax_weights(q, beta)` active weights.
- `log_update(theta, grad_theta, lr, momentum... )`.
- `optimize_sequence(p0, budget, seed)`:
  * n = len(p0), Nq, Nw.
  * Start with normalized p.
  * If n under/over, maybe adapt: choose target n from parent length or 4096/8192.
  * Maintain best p, best fft score.
  * Run phases:
    1. Early moderate beta (20-100) with Adam for 20s to smooth.
    2. Increase beta gradually to 2000; use line search; maybe exact active uniform.
    3. Random restarts/perturbations from best.
  * Periodically (or at end) compute exact score using evaluate_sequence (if n<=10000). Use as best exact to avoid FFT errors and return exact best.
- `maybe improve parent by reducing length?` We can downsample parent if too long to final exact. But parent length 4806 okay. Use parent as one start.
- Also optimize from **one-sided power** length 8192; maybe coordinate gradient can push to ~1.5.
- Use **adaptive n selection**: We can run optimization on several lengths: 2048, 4096, 8192; evaluate exact/FFT, keep best. Time split: maybe 800s each. But more lengths may find better. Since final exact score O(n^2), keep n<=8192 for exact. But if hidden eval can handle larger, maybe n=16384 could be better. We can choose based on exact data. To be safe, final length <=8192. The previous parent 4806; can keep 8192.
- Memory: gradient FFT Nw ~ next fast len(3n) ~32768 for n8192; fine.

Need optimize runtime. Numpy operations per iteration:
   q = irfft(A*A,Nq) length Nq, take 2n-1, max.
   w = exp(beta*(q/max-1)); w=sum normalized length 2n-1.
   w_rev = w[::-1]
   P = rfft(p,Nw) (p length n); Wr = rfft(w_rev,Nw)
   T = irfft(P*Wr,Nw); h = T[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
   g=2h; gradθ=p*(g - dot(p,g))
   Adam update θ.
   p = softmax.
Now score new p requires recompute q each step. So 2 FFT irfft + 2 rfft? We can maybe combine: We need A=rfft(p,Nq) and P=rfft(p,Nw). Use next_fast_len(3n-2) which is also ≥2n-1; one N=Nw can be used for q too by zeroing? If Nw≥2n-1, conv length 2n-1 within Nw. So use a single N = next_fast_len(3n-2) for both p and q. But rfft(p,N) once; pft=q_conv=irfft(P*P,N) -> q. For gradient, pft=W? use P and Wr. Good: one rfft(p,N), one rfft(w_rev,N), two irffts. That's 4 FFTs per iteration. For N=32768, maybe ~0.5ms each? Python overhead maybe 5-10ms. 200k steps possible. But need manage time.

Can we compute gradient more cheaply using q smooth? Since w is a function of q. But no.

Can use **complex FFT** and avoid rfft? not needed.

- Use **Nesterov-like momentum** in log space. Adam enough.
- Use **step size scheduling**: Start lr high (0.5), reduce when score stagnates. Adaptive line search: Try a list of lr multipliers (1,0.5,0.25) to find improvement. Since gradient can oscillate, use backtracking to maintain descent.
- Use **two warm-started bets**: beta low for global shaping, high for finishing.
- Use **reweighting based on actual qmax**: The objective is max. If gradient descent with active masks, after a step some non-active peaks become max. Active masks adapt.
- Use **exact max scoring**: score = 2n*max(q). The smooth weights approximate. We can also compute **score gradient** exactly at active set: w uniform over indices where q==qmax (within floating) maybe multiple. But due finite, exact max may be a single peak; uniform over top few.
- Use **penalizing all peaks**: Instead of pure active, use a mixture of active set and full softmax to avoid overfitting.

Algorithm for an initial candidate p:
- Normalize, maybe clip zeros to tiny? Since optimizer uses log, ensure positive min 1e-300.
- Evaluate score (FFT). Keep top candidates.

Optimization loop per candidate:
```
def optimize_candidate(p0, time_limit, beta_start=30, beta_end=1000, seed):
    p = normalize(p0)
    theta = log(p) - logsumexp
    best = p, score
    beta = beta_start
    lr = 0.3
    state...
    while time < limit:
       compute q, score
       if score < best: update
       compute weights active: mask q >= qmax*(1 - 1/(20*beta))? maybe use softmax
       grad
       step with backtracking:
          for trial in lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25:

```
def compute_feedback(p, N, active_count):
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P*P, N)[:2*n-1]
    # clip negative to 0
    if q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0)
    qmax = q.max()
    score=2*n*qmax
    if active_count >= q.size:
       w = np.full(q.size, 1/q.size)
    else:
       idx = np.argpartition(q, -active_count)[-active_count:]
       w = np.zeros_like(q)
       # weights maybe softmax on top values to emphasize larger within top
       top = q[idx]
       topmax = top.max()
       # If topmax >0:
       y = top/topmax  # 1 at max, slightly lower
       # smooth: exp(beta_active*(y-1)) with beta_active perhaps 10
       beta_act = 10
       vals = np.exp(beta_act*(y-1))
       # add tiny to all top? uniform if underflow
       vals = np.where(vals>0, vals, 1e-12)
       w[idx] = vals/vals.sum()
       # But if idx size active_count, top=top values not sorted; okay.
    # compute T = cross_corr(w,p)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    T = np.fft.irfft(P*Wr, N)
    L = 2*n-2
    h = T[L-active_count? wait length n]
```
`h_i = T[L - i]`; i=0..n-1. So `h = T[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]`. Ensure L+1 within N; N >=3n-2, T length N. If N larger, okay. If using N=next_fast_len(3n-2).

def adam_step(dnat, lr, state):
    m,v,t=...

def optimize_sequence(p0, budget_s, seed=0, target_n=None):
    if budget_s <=0: return p0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
    p=normalize_nonneg(p0)
    n=p.size
    if target_n is not None and n != target_n and target_n>=64:

For real sequences, yes FFT of convolution = product (not conjugate). Standard convolution theorem: FFT(f*g)=FFT(f)*FFT(g). So correct. Now, initial sequences. Central binomial recurrence:
```
def central_sequence(n):
    a=np.ones(n); cur=1.0
    for k in range(1,n): cur *= (2*k-1)/(2*k); a[k]=cur
    return a/sum
```
This is binom(2k-2? Wait k index? For k=1, coefficient C(2,1)/4=0.5. Recurrence from a0=1: a_{k}=a_{k-1}*(2k-1)/(2k). Yes.

x=np.arange(n); a=(x+1)**(-alpha); normalize
```

Now, exact score direct:
```
def exact_score_array(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.maximum(p,0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    b=np.convolve(p,p)
    mx=b.max()
    val=2*len(p)*mx/(s*s)
    return val if finite else inf
```

Pseudo code for `run_optimizer`:
```
def run_optimizer(p0, budget_s, seed=0, exact_iterations=None):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
    p=normalize_nonneg(p0); n=p.size
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]
    if q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0)
    score=2*n*q.max()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    theta=log_weights(p)
    best_theta=theta.copy()
    # Adam state
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=0.1
    reject=0
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    phase=0
    phases_active = [max(128,n//8), max(64,n//24), max(32,n//64), max(12,n//256), max(8,n//1024)]
    phases_beta = [10,20,40,80,120]
    while time < deadline-2:
        phase = min(4, int((time-monotonic?))) maybe transition based on time: after 120s phase1, 300 phase2, 600 phase3, 1000 phase4? Or stall based.
        active=phases_active[phase]
        P current; q,score
        if score < best_score: update best_theta
        # weights
        if active >= q.size: w = ones/size
        else:
            idx=np.argpartition(q, -active)[-active:]
            w=zeros; top=q[idx]; topmax=top.max()
            if topmax>0:
                y=top/topmax -1
                vals=np.exp(min(50, phases_beta[phase])*y)
                if not finite/sum==0: vals=ones
            else: vals=ones
            w[idx]=vals/vals.sum()
        # gradient
        T = cross_corr_weights_p(w,p,P? Could use P and Wr. P current may be stale? p current yes. We can recompute Wr each iter.
        Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
        conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Wr,N)
        L=2*n-2
        h=conv[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
        grad=2*h
        dtheta=p*(grad - np.dot(p,grad))
        # Adam
        t+=1
        m=0.9m+0.1dtheta; v=0.999v+0.001dtheta^2
        mw=m/(1-0.9**t); vv=v/(1-0.999**t)
        step=mw/(sqrt(vv)+1e-9)
        step=np.clip(step,-2,2)
        # Try step sizes maybe 1,0.5
        accepted=False
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
             theta_try=theta - lr*mult*step
             p_try=softmax(theta_try)
             P_try=np.fft.rfft(p_try,N)
             q_try=np.fft.irfft(P_try*P_try,N)[:2*n-1]
             if q_try.min()<0: q_try=np.maximum(q_try,0)
             sc_try=2*n*q_try.max()
             if sc_try + 1e-12 < score:
                  accept; break
             if sc_try < best_score + 0.01 and rng.random()<0.1: accept? maybe not.
        if not accepted:
             reject +=1
             if reject >= 20:
                lr=max(0.01,lr*0.8)
                if reject>=100:
                     # reset to best with noise
                     theta=best_theta + rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
                     m=v=zeros; lr=0.1; reject=0
                     recompute P,q,score
                continue
        else:
             reject=0
             if sc_try < score: lr=min(0.25,lr*1.02)
        if time.last_best > 300:
             # strong restart
             sig = 0.05 + 0.1*min(5,(time-last_best)/300)
             theta=best_theta + rng.normal(0,sig,n)
             reset state, recompute
    recompute best exact? return best_p
```

Detailed functions:
```
def safe_nonneg(arr): ...
def normalize_sum1(arr): ...
def log_weights(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
    w=np.log(a)
    w-=w.max()
    return w
def softmax(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0)
    w-=w.max()
    e=np.exp(w)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(w))/len(w)
    return e/s
```
`add_top_candidate(top, p, score, max=8)` top list of (score,p).

def run_optimizer(p0, budget_s, seed=0, quiet=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(1.0,budget_s)
    p=normalize_sum1(p0)
    n=p.size
    if n<16: n=2048; p=normalize_sum1(central_sequence(n))
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]
    if q.size and q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0)
    score=2.0*n*q.max()
    if not finite: reset to central
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    theta=log_weights(p); best_theta=theta.copy()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=0.08
    reject=0
    last_best=start

        (60, max(64,n//8), 1),
        (180, max(32,n//24), 3),
        (420, max(16,n//64), 5),
        (900, max(8,n//256), 7),

```
def subproblem_step(p, N, iters=20, seed):
   b=normalize_sum1(p)
   theta_b=log_weights(b)
   P_p=rfft(p,N)
   for _ in range(iters):
       B=rfft(b,N)
       h=irfft(P_p*B,N)[:2n-1] # p*b
       # weights top active from h
       # gradient wrt b: for fixed p, F=Σw (p*b), gradient = 2? derivative =? T_i = Σ_j p_j w_{i+j}; not 2 (since one b). Check F=Σ w_k Σ_j p_j b_{k-j}; derivative b_i = Σ_j p_j w_{i+j}, no factor 2. Use grad=T.
       T = cross_corr(p,w) (same function with p). Natural grad.
       Adam update theta_b.
   return b
```
Then for mix t in (0.5,1.0), candidate norm((1-t)p+t b), evaluate score. If improves p, accept.

Interpolation function:
```
def resample_sequence(arr, target):
    arr=normalize_sum1(arr)
    n=arr.size
    if target==n: return arr
    if target<1: return arr
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,target)
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(arr,1e-300))
    lognew=np.interp(new, old, loga)
    return softmax(lognew)
```
For downsample large, linear interp may average. Good.

def recompute_state(p,N):
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*len(p)-1]
    if q.size:
       if q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0)
       score=2.0*len(p)*float(np.max(q))
    else: score=inf
    return P,q,score
```
But function returns three; okay.

`top_weights(q, active, power)`:
```
def top_weights(q, active, power):
    L=q.size
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if active>=L:
       w.fill(1/L); return w
    if active<1: active=1
    idx=np.argpartition(q, -active)[-active:]
    top=q[idx]
    topmax=float(np.max(top))
    if topmax <=0 or not finite:
        w[idx]=1/active
        return w
    ratio=top/topmax
    ratio=np.where(ratio>0, ratio, 1e-30)
    vals=np.power(ratio, power)
    if not finite or sum<=0: vals=np.ones(active)
    w[idx]=vals/vals.sum()
    return w
```
If power=pow int, values positive. For top ratios near 1, okay. For top lower, small.

`cross_corr_weights_p(P,p,w,N,n)`:
```
def cross_corr_weights_p(P,w,N,n):
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Wr,N)
    L=2*n-2
    h=conv[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
    return h
```
Need P current = rfft(p,N). If p changed but P not updated, bug. We maintain.

Let's proceed to final code. (In final, only code block.)
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

with an FFT-based smoothed-Linf descent in log-probability space.

The optimizer keeps the sequence on the simplex (sum = 1) using a
log-softmax parametrization, repeatedly selects a small active set of
large convolution coefficients, computes the corresponding subgradient,
and takes Adam-style steps with simple line-search/restarts.  Several
different starting families are tried (provided previous answer,
power laws, a flat-frequency square-root heuristic, and noisy variants),
and the best FFT candidate is periodically / finally validated with a
direct convolution.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

# Global safety limits: direct convolution for final validation can get
# expensive, but 8192 is still comfortable on one CPU.
MAX_N = 8192
MIN_N = 1024

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", divide="ignore", invalid="ignore")


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_sum1(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative vector normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(8, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    return arr / s


def log_weights(p):
    """Centered log weights of a positive simplex vector."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    lw = np.log(p)
    lw = np.where(np.isfinite(lw), lw, 0.0)
    return lw - float(np.max(lw))


def softmax_from_log(lw):
    """Stable softmax in log space."""
    lw = np.asarray(lw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    lw = np.where(np.isfinite(lw), lw, 0.0)
    lw -= float(np.max(lw))
    e = np.exp(lw)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(1, lw.size), dtype=np.float64) / float(lw.size)
    return e / s


def fft_score(p, N):
    """Score 2*n*max(conv(p,p)) for sum(p)=1 using circular FFT."""
    n = p.size
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    if c.size:
        if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def recompute(p, N):
    """Return P, convolution coefficients, and score for current p."""
    n = p.size
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    if c.size:
        if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        score = float("inf")
    else:
        score = float(2.0 * n * mx)
    return P, c, score


def top_weights(q, active, power):
    """
    Weights on a small active set of large convolution coefficients.

    active  : number of top coefficients to include
    power   : strength of emphasizing larger active values
              weights proportional to (q_top / qmax)**power
    """
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = q.size
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return w
    if active >= L:
        w.fill(1.0 / L)
        return w
    if active < 1:
        active = 1

    idx = np.argpartition(q, -active)[-active:]
    top = q[idx]
    topmax = float(np.max(top))
    if not np.isfinite(topmax) or topmax <= 0.0:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(active)
        return w

    ratio = top / topmax
    ratio = np.where(ratio > 0.0, ratio, 1e-30)
    vals = np.power(ratio, float(power))
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        vals = np.ones(active, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(active)
    w[idx] = vals / s
    return w


def cross_corr_weights_p(P, w, N, n):
    """
    For fixed weights w_k on convolution coefficients, compute
        h_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j},  i=0..n-1
    where P = rfft(p, N).
    """
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(P * Wr, N)
    L = 2 * n - 2
    h = conv[L - n + 1 : L + 1][::-1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def exact_score_array(p):
    """Exact O(n^2) score using numpy.convolve, for final validation."""
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def central_sequence(n):
    """Binomial-like coefficients C(2k,k)/4^k, normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cur = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        cur *= (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
        a[k] = cur
    return normalize_sum1(a)


def power_sequence(n, alpha):
    """(i+1)^(-alpha), normalized."""
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    a = np.power(x, -float(alpha))
    return normalize_sum1(a)


def exp_sequence(n, lam):
    """exp(lam * i/(n-1)), normalized."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.exp(float(lam) * t)
    return normalize_sum1(a)


def linear_sequence(n, slope):
    """1 + slope * i/(n-1), normalized, clipped nonnegative."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(slope) * t
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return normalize_sum1(a)


def flat_root_sequence(n):
    """
    Heuristic start from a square root of a flat target polynomial.

    Target q_k = 1 on all 2n-1 coefficients.  Take magnitude/phase half
    in Fourier space, recover a real vector, then project to nonnegative
    coefficients.  This is not theoretically optimal, but gives a strong
    different basin.
    """
    N = next_fast_len(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    q = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    q[: 2 * n - 1] = 1.0
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(Q))
    ang = 0.5 * np.angle(Q)
    A = np.fft.irfft(mag * np.exp(1j * ang), N)
    a = A[:n]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.abs(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = central_sequence(n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, target):
    """Resample a positive simplex sequence to target length in log domain."""
    a = normalize_sum1(a)
    n = a.size
    if target == n:
        return a
    if target < 1:
        return a
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    logb = np.interp(new_x, old_x, loga)
    return softmax_from_log(logb)


def load_parent():
    """Load height_sequence_1 if available."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size <= 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    return arr


def add_candidate(cands, p, score):
    """Keep a small list of candidates ordered by FFT score (worst replaceable)."""
    if p is None:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = float("inf")
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return
    if len(cands) < 12:
        cands.append((float(score), arr.copy()))
        return
    worst = 0
    worst_sc = float(cands[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(cands)):
        si = float(cands[i][0])
        if si > worst_sc:
            worst_sc = si
            worst = i
    if score < worst_sc:
        cands[worst] = (float(score), arr.copy())


def run_optimizer(p0, budget_s, seed=0, start_phase=0, tag="opt"):
    """
    Local optimizer for one start.  Returns (best_p, best_fft_score).
    """
    p0 = normalize_sum1(p0)
    if p0.size < 8:
        p0 = central_sequence(MAX_N)

    n0 = p0.size
    if n0 > MAX_N:
        p0 = resample_sequence(p0, MAX_N)
    elif n0 < MIN_N:
        p0 = resample_sequence(p0, MIN_N)

    n = p0.size
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)

    P, q, score = recompute(p0, N)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        p0 = central_sequence(n)
        P, q, score = recompute(p0, N)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return p0, float("inf")

    p = p0.copy()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    theta = log_weights(p)
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.05
    reject_cnt = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    active_denominators = [8, 20, 50, 160, 700]
    powers = [1, 2, 4, 6, 9]

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    last_best = start
    last_print = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        if (not np.isfinite(score) or
                not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or
                not np.all(np.isfinite(theta))):
            p = best_p.copy()
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            P, q, score = recompute(p, N)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.05
            reject_cnt = 0
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break
            continue

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if elapsed < 60.0:
            extra = 0
        elif elapsed < 180.0:
            extra = 1
        elif elapsed < 420.0:
            extra = 2
        elif elapsed < 800.0:
            extra = 3
        else:
            extra = 4
        phase = min(4, int(start_phase) + extra)

        # If we are far behind the best or stagnant, restore best + noise.
        if score > best_score + 0.05 or (time.monotonic() - last_best > 240.0):
            sig = 0.03 + 0.08 * min(4.0, (time.monotonic() - last_best) / 120.0)
            theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, float(sig), size=n)
            p = softmax_from_log(theta)
            P, q, score = recompute(p, N)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.05
            reject_cnt = 0
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                p = best_p.copy()
                theta = best_theta.copy()
                P, q, score = recompute(p, N)
                if not np.isfinite(score):
                    break
                continue
            continue

        active = max(16, n // active_denominators[phase])
        power = float(powers[phase])

        w = top_weights(q, active, power)
        h = cross_corr_weights_p(P, w, N, n)
        grad = 2.0 * h
        avg = float(np.dot(p, grad))
        dtheta = p * (grad - avg)
        dtheta = np.where(np.isfinite(dtheta), dtheta, 0.0)

        # Guard against pathological near-zero vectors.
        if float(np.sum(p * p)) <= 1e-300:
            p = best_p.copy()
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            P, q, score = recompute(p, N)
            continue

        # Proposed Adam update compute.
        m1 = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * dtheta
        v1 = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (dtheta * dtheta)
        t1 = t + 1
        mw = m1 / (1.0 - 0.9 ** t1)
        vv = v1 / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t1)
        step = mw / (np.sqrt(vv) + 1e-12)
        step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
        step = np.clip(step, -4.0, 4.0)

        old_score = float(score)
        accepted = False
        p_try = None
        P_try = None
        q_try = None
        sc_try = float("inf")
        theta_try = theta

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5):
            theta_try = theta - lr * mult * step
            theta_try = np.where(np.isfinite(theta_try), theta_try, 0.0)
            p_try = softmax_from_log(theta_try)
            P_try = np.fft.rfft(p_try, N)
            q_try = np.fft.irfft(P_try * P_try, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
            if q_try.size:
                if float(np.min(q_try)) < 0.0:
                    q_try = np.maximum(q_try, 0.0)
                sc_try = float(2.0 * n * np.max(q_try))
            else:
                sc_try = float("inf")

            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-10 < old_score:
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            # Commit state.
            theta = theta_try
            p = p_try.copy()
            P = P_try
            q = q_try
            score = sc_try
            m = m1
            v = v1
            t = t1
            reject_cnt = 0

            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = float(score)
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_theta = theta.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()

            # Mild adaptive LR.
            if score < old_score * 0.999:
                lr = min(0.20, lr * 1.03)
            else:
                lr = max(0.015, lr * 0.99)
        else:
            reject_cnt += 1
            if reject_cnt >= 6:
                lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.85)
            if reject_cnt >= 45:
                # Force a fresh trial from the best with a small noise.
                sig = 0.05 + 0.10 * min(4.0, reject_cnt / 45.0)
                theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, float(sig), size=n)
                p = softmax_from_log(theta)
                P, q, score = recompute(p, N)
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t = 0
                lr = 0.05
                reject_cnt = 0
                if not np.isfinite(score):
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    theta = best_theta.copy()
                    P, q, score = recompute(p, N)

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 45.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] t={time.monotonic() - start:8.2f}s "
                  f"phase={phase} active={active} "
                  f"cur={score:.9f} best_fft={best_score:.9f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    # Return the best by FFT score.
    return normalize_sum1(best_p), best_score


def finalize_sequence(p):
    """Scale to sum sqrt(2n), ensure non-negative finite list."""
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        a = a * (scale / s)
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (scale / float(n))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 < 0.01:
        a = a * (0.01 / max(s2, 1e-300))
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the harness.

    Runs a multi-start smoothed-Linf search and returns a non-negative
    sequence, scaled to sum approx sqrt(2n).
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(max(60.0, budget), 2386.0)

    outer_start = time.monotonic()
    outer_deadline = outer_start + budget

    print("Starting search, effective budget =", f"{budget:.2f}s")

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is None:
        n_main = 4096
        parent = central_sequence(n_main)
        n_parent = n_main
    else:
        parent = normalize_sum1(parent)
        n_parent = int(parent.size)
        if n_parent > MAX_N:
            parent = resample_sequence(parent, MAX_N)
            n_parent = MAX_N
        elif n_parent < MIN_N:
            parent = resample_sequence(parent, MIN_N)
            n_parent = MIN_N
        n_main = n_parent

    # A second length gives more "attack surface" when appropriate.
    n_second = MAX_N if (n_main != MAX_N and n_main >= 2048) else n_main

    # Build a pool of starts.
    raw_starts = []

    # Parent at native length and possibly resampled.
    raw_starts.append(parent)
    if n_second != n_main:
        raw_starts.append(resample_sequence(parent, n_second))

    # Deterministic candidate families.
    for ns, length in ((0, n_main), (1, n_second)):
        for alpha in (0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60):
            raw_starts.append(power_sequence(length, alpha))
        for lam in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
            raw_starts.append(exp_sequence(length, lam))
        for slope in (-1.5, -0.75, 0.75, 1.5):
            raw_starts.append(linear_sequence(length, slope))
        raw_starts.append(flat_root_sequence(length))
        raw_starts.append(central_sequence(length))

    # A few noisy variants of the parent.
    rng_init = np.random.default_rng(seed + 777)
    for sig in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
        lw = log_weights(parent) + rng_init.normal(0.0, float(sig), size=parent.size)
        raw_starts.append(softmax_from_log(lw))

    # Quick score all starts and keep top three.
    scored_starts = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(raw_starts):
        p = normalize_sum1(p)
        if p.size < 16 or p.size > MAX_N:
            continue
        Nq = next_fast_len(3 * p.size - 2)
        sc = fft_score(p, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_starts.append((sc, p))

    if not scored_starts:
        scored_starts = [(fft_score(parent, next_fast_len(3 * parent.size - 2)), parent)]

    # Sort by FFT score.
    n_start_pool = len(scored_starts)
    scores_pool = np.zeros(n_start_pool, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n_start_pool):
        scores_pool[i] = float(scored_starts[i][0])
    order = np.argsort(scores_pool)

    top_starts = []
    for j in order:
        top_starts.append(scored_starts[int(j)][1])
    top_starts = top_starts[:3]
    while len(top_starts) < 3:
        top_starts.append(parent)

    # Exact baseline for the provided parent (if time allows).
    best_exact_p = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")
    if time.monotonic() < outer_deadline - 300.0 and parent.size <= MAX_N:
        print("Evaluating parent exactly...")
        sc_parent = exact_score_array(parent)
        print("parent exact score =", f"{sc_parent:.9f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_parent) and sc_parent < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = sc_parent
            best_exact_p = parent.copy()

    cands = []
    add_candidate(cands, parent, best_exact_score if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) else fft_score(parent, next_fast_len(3 * parent.size - 2)))

    best_fft_p = parent.copy()
    best_fft_score = best_exact_score if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) else fft_score(parent, next_fast_len(3 * parent.size - 2))

    fractions = [0.50, 0.30, 0.20]

    for k, start_p in enumerate(top_starts):
        rem = outer_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        alloc = float(fractions[k]) * budget
        alloc = max(30.0, min(alloc, rem - 180.0))
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break

        ph = [0, 1, 2, 3][k] if k < 4 else 3
        print(f"Optimizing start {k+1}, length={start_p.size},"
              f" time={alloc:.2f}s, start_phase={ph}")

        res_p, res_sc = run_optimizer(start_p, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 1000 + 17 * (k + 1),
                                      start_phase=ph, tag=f"s{k+1}")

        if not np.isfinite(res_sc):
            res_sc = fft_score(res_p, next_fast_len(3 * res_p.size - 2))

        add_candidate(cands, res_p, res_sc)
        if res_sc < best_fft_score:
            best_fft_score = res_sc
            best_fft_p = res_p.copy()

        # Exact validation for this candidate if budget permits.
        rem = outer_deadline - time.monotonic()
        needed = 120.0 if res_p.size > 6000 else 60.0
        if rem > needed:
            scx = exact_score_array(res_p)
            if np.isfinite(scx):
                print(f"[s{k+1}] exact score =", f"{scx:.9f}")
                if scx < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = scx
                    best_exact_p = res_p.copy()
        else:
            print(f"[s{k+1}] skipping exact, remaining={rem:.2f}s")

    # Final exact validation of a few best FFT candidates.
    if cands:
        nc = len(cands)
        cs = np.zeros(nc, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(nc):
            cs[i] = float(cands[i][0])
        ord_c = np.argsort(cs)
        evaluated = 0
        for idx in ord_c:
            rem = outer_deadline - time.monotonic()
            arr_len = int(cands[int(idx)][1].size)
            cost_thresh = 100.0 if arr_len > 6000 else 40.0
            if rem < cost_thresh or evaluated >= 4:
                break
            arr = cands[int(idx)][1]
            scx = exact_score_array(arr)
            evaluated += 1
            if np.isfinite(scx):
                print("final exact candidate score =", f"{scx:.9f}")
                if scx < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = scx
                    best_exact_p = arr.copy()

    # Choose result: exact if available, otherwise FFT best.
    if best_exact_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_exact_score):
        result = best_exact_p
        print("Using exact best score =", f"{best_exact_score:.9f}")
    else:
        result = best_fft_p
        print("Using FFT best score =", f"{best_fft_score:.9f}")

    return finalize_sequence(result)
```
